### Stations +425.0 to +420.0

- **small empty boat (sampan) moored under the small bridge** `S1.veh.boat_sampan_36440`: station +424.3, 6.0 m S; H -0.7 m (stream); 1.3 m SW of small plank bridge (草桥); moored; faces E; 2.97 m long. A small open wooden boat (a sampan), empty: no crew, mast, cabin, sweep or cargo. It lies along the bank just south of the small bridge, a raised square end (transom) to the west and a raised pointed end to the east, with one thwart near the east end. A thin upright pole stands in the stream at its pointed east end (S1.prop.mooring_pole_36444): the boat is tied to it. (sections.md §2: mist, sparse trees and water at the very start) Nearest: upright pole at the small boat's east end (mooring pole?) 1.6 m E, tall straight bare tree at the bridge's west end 3.2 m W, big bare elm-like tree at the small bridge's east end 5.2 m E. [est: rear, beam_n, beam_s; identification med; faded] Note: Outline only, in thin lines: a vertical stroke for the west end's transom (u 36175-36183, v 1131-1150); the far gunwale (36180, 1132) -> (36293, 1157) -> (36440, 1180); the near gunwale (36193, 1160) -> (36327, 1190) -> (36437, 1190); the bottom's curve (36353, 1210) -> (36440, 1187) up to the pointed end; a thwart tick at (36397, 1175-1180). SCALE CUE: hull 262 px long and 30-40 px deep, at v 1130-1210. front = the pointed east end, which the line ties to the bridge (S1.str.small_bridge_36365, a post foot at about (36445, 1100)); no rudder or sweep shows, so which end is the bow is a reading (conf low on it). BOTH READINGS of the water: afloat in the stream at the bank's edge (support 'stream', taken: the mooring line, and the plain silk round it is the same as under the bridge) or drawn up on the bank (the bank's wash strokes run under its west half, (36207, 1180) -> (36333, 1227)). The restyle draws it about 30 px south-east of the outline, on dry ground with grass: not used. No crew, so no group. MERGE: the rope's far end is the foot of a post of S1.str.small_bridge_36365 (its bays, at about (36445, 1100)). No rudder, sweep or side sweeps are painted, so the lead's bow rule (rudder or sweep = rear) cannot be applied: front stays the pointed east end tied to the bridge, conf low. SECOND PASS (start): AFLOAT, confirmed. The hull lies wholly on pale silk. Its near gunwale (36193, 1160) -> (36327, 1190) runs 20-35 px north of S1.grd.spit_under_boat_36140's north edge (36250, 1210) -> (36335, 1228). The dark stroke under its west half, (36207, 1180) -> (36327, 1223), is that spit's edge, not ground under the boat. The west transom (36173-36187, 1127-1160) is about 70 px east of the west bank's foot (S1.grd.bank_36060: (36105, 1110), (36075, 1180)). Support stream stands. BOW: still unreadable. No rudder, sweep or side sweep is drawn; the west end is a raised square transom and the east end lower and pointed. front = east stays, conf low. THE LINE at its east end is vertical at u 36442-36446, v about 1125-1180. It stops 40-60 px below the nearest bridge post foot (36455, 1063), and no post stands at (36445, 1100). It is proposed as its own prop, S1.prop.mooring_pole_36444 (a pole in the stream bed the boat is tied to). If accepted, read 'the line' in this note and in `what` as that pole.

Big bare elm-like tree (form elm_claw) with two stems from one gnarled, knotted foot on the north bank of the stream (two dark knot-holes on the lower trunk): the west stem rises straight and bends over in a great arched limb; the east stem leans steeply north-east and crosses the trunk of the tall tree beside it; dark crab-claw twigs, no leaves. (sections.md S1: mist, sparse trees and water at the very start (≈ 35000–37610)) Nearest: big bare elm reaching west over the grove 1.1 m N, tall straight bare tree at the bridge's west end 2.7 m E, small empty boat (sampan) moored under the small bridge 5.9 m E. West stem: u 35966-36001 at v 790-1056, then curving west to (35905-35940, 650), arching limb with its apex at (35986-36036, 444), twigs to v about 405 (top). East stem: from (35966, 1134) by (36016, 1027) and (36051, 949) to (36087, 849), where it crosses S1.tree.bare_elm_tall_36070's trunk. Beyond the crossing it is lost among that tree's limbs. Reading 2: it bends upright and becomes the tall trunk itself (then this tree's top is about (36115, 290) and the tall tree's own trunk ends at the crossing). Read as one tree because both stems leave one knotted foot; two trees with touching feet is possible (the restyle draws two). Painted height about 795 px. SUPPORT: its foot is on the face of the stream's west bank (structures_e's S1.grd.bank_36060 / bank_35200's east end): bank top about v 1150-1178 above it, the bank's foot and the water about v 1240-1255 below it; its roots spread on the bank (35850-36000, v 1180-1230).

### Stations +415.0 to +410.0

- **pale far willow crown behind the long farmhouse** `S1.tree.far_willow_34995`: station +415.0, 5.0 m N; 0 m NE of long farmhouse behind the barn (west gable); about 2.05 m tall; crown 1.62 m wide. A small fan of long thin shoots drawn in pale grey ink, rising up and out to the north-west from a point just above the long farmhouse's ridge and the far roof's east slope: a far pollarded willow (willow_fan form, drawn small and pale for distance) standing behind the farmstead, its head and trunk hidden by the roofs. (余辉: 寂静的谷场) Nearest: far willow crown above the farmyard willow (uncertain) 1.7 m SE, pollarded willow over the threshing-floor shed 3.4 m S, long low plank (bench or trough) under the shed 3.8 m SW. The fan is drawn in the same pale grey wash as the far roof, so it stands far back. BOTH READINGS: a separate far willow (this entity), or the near willow's west shoots drawn pale (then decline this, and the near willow's note stands). The restyle also paints a separate pale tree here (u 34900-35020, v 490-600); that is a hint, not evidence.

[est: base; identification low; part: lower half hidden by the big thatched roof and two tree trunks] Note: Outline: west side 34062 from v 705 down to 765, top (34078-34095, 697), east end behind S1.tree.elm_34110's trunk at about 34100. The restyle draws a thatched roof. If structures_e records it as a building, drop this prop. MERGE: structures_e recorded the same dome as the building S1.bld.small_round_roof_34080 (dark hatched dome u 34064-34097, v 700-725; a thin vertical stroke at u ~34066 down to v ~745 that may be a post; readings: a small round thatched shelter on a post, or the top of a straw stack); that building is retired and this prop kept. On the merge's zoom-2 crop the dome has a smooth top, a straight west side and no eave fringe or wall under it, which reads as a stack. If the second pass finds the post and an eave, it becomes a small shelter again (a building). SECOND PASS (farms): no post or eave found (dome_n.jpg zoom 3, dome_hp.jpg zoom 4, roller_z4.jpg). The dome's top arc runs from (34063, 715) over (34085, 700) to (34100, 698). Its west side comes down to (34063, 745), where it meets the east hip slope of S1.bld.farmhouse_thatched_33955 (34060, 743) -> (34107, 790). Below the arc there is no open space and no shaft: the 'vertical stroke at ~34066 down to v ~745' is the dome's own west side ending on the slope. Its foot is hidden behind the big house's roof. It stays a stack (or the top of a roof behind the house), conf low.

### Stations +370.0 to +365.0

The largest tree of the countryside: a massive, dark, lumpy and moss-dotted bole 150-200 px wide with exposed knobbly roots running east along the ground to about u 32845; the bole leans west as it rises to a broad knobbly pollard head of two or three clubbed knobs (u 32400-32600, v 930-1055), from which a fountain of long straight bare whips rises to the top of the crown (v ≈ 290) and spreads from about 32230 to 32790. Crown attribution: the high fine fans at v 290-530 (u 32265-32600) and v 465-650 (u 32530-32800) are read as this tree's whip tips (no other head or trunk is painted under them); they could hide far willow heads, but none shows. Painted height ≈ 1300 px (1588 -> 290). SCALE CUE: bole about 150 px wide at v 1500. SECOND PASS (willows), the flagged 'roots or a rock': the knobbly mass east of B's foot is a low lobe from u 32723 to 32860. It has a pale lit face on its west part (32750-32773, 1553-1583) and dense dark knobs and dots on its east part. Between the trunk's own flare (it sweeps down into the ground at about 32705-32740, v 1582-1600) and the lobe there is only a narrow pale gap above v 1585. Kept as B's exposed roots. The burled willow S1.tree.willow_great_burl_32065 has its root lobes drawn in the same way (pale-topped lobes with dark knobbed edges at 32057-32128, v 1455-1500 and 32152-32286, v 1400-1500), and the map already reads those as roots. ### Stations +355.0 to +350.0

- **great willow leaning across the hut's fence (hollow bole)** `S1.tree.willow_leaning_hut_31240`: station +351.4, 0.4 m N; 3 m S of tall wattle fence east of the hut's gateway; about 12.1 m tall; crown 13.51 m wide. A great old willow at the east end of the thatched hut, leaning hard north-west: its huge low hollow bole (a broad mottled mass about 150 px wide with a dark round hollow in its east face) sits on the bank just south of the shed's fence, and its trunk runs up-left past the west end of that fence, in front of the hut's wattle fence and east gable, to a pollard head at about the hut's eave, from which bare whips fan up over the hut's thatched roof. Early spring, bare. willow_burl form. (余辉: 隋堤烟柳) Nearest: A-frame trestle in the hut's fence opening 4.5 m NW, great willow leaning north-west (west of the four) 7.9 m E, large jar with a tied cover, under the shed on posts 8.2 m NE. [est: base; identification med; part: trunk crosses in front of the fences; head and crown mixed with the hut's roof and the willow whips over it] Note: Bole extents about u 31150-31310, v 1330-1480, the hollow at (31260-31270, 1365-1375), its top at (31270, 1335); its foot merges with the dark wash of a bank at v 1405-1480, so the base is est. The trunk: a dark diagonal from the bole at (31150, 1345) to (31100, 1300), then a pale band with a dark west edge at u 31010-31100, v 1190-1330 (structures_w independently reads 'the leaning trunk of a big willow' here, in front of the fence at u 31017-31087), up to the eave at about (31000-31060, 1120-1150); the head is lost in the roof's hatching (the restyle puts a knobbly head at (30850-30920, 1020-1100)), and the fans of whips over the roof (u 30950-31330, v 550-1000) are taken as its crown, so top and crown points are est. Readings: (a, kept) one great leaning willow rooted in the bole (the restyle draws exactly this); (b) the bole is a separate cut stump or a rock on the bank, and the trunk at u 31010-31100 belongs to a tree rooted at the fence's foot at about (31060, 1340) (t2's range), whose crown is the same whips. Under (b) the trunk's tree is t2's and the bole is a stump (mine) or a rock (structures_w's). The fence strokes above the bole (u 31120-31285, v 1100-1310) are drawn whole, so nothing rises in front of the fence's east part. Settle at the second pass on a zoom-3 read of u 31000-31320, v 1100-1490. MERGE: crown_w and top moved to t2's reading (crown_w (30290, 1050), top (30780, 560), both est; were (30900, 950) and (31100, 600)): on a zoom-0.7 crop of u 30200-31400 the pollard head is at about (30860-30990, 990-1120), the trunk runs unbroken from it down-right in front of the hut's fence into the hollow bole, and its whips fan west over the farmhouse to u ~30290 and up to v ~560. t1's "willow crown over the farmhouse's east end, trunk east of 30300" (u 30290-30460, v 800-1190) is this crown. Reading (a) looks right at this zoom: conf raised to med; the second pass confirms at zoom 3. SECOND PASS (west), the flagged zoom-3 read of u 31000-31320, v 1100-1490: reading (a) holds. On zoom 1.6 and 2.5 crops the trunk is one band: from the bole's upper west side (31150, 1345) diagonal strokes run up-left to the fence's foot at (31046-31102, 1297-1335); a pale band with a dark west edge rises at u 31008-31046 from v 1310 to the hut's eave (v ~1122); above the eave a dark trunk section at u 30902-30940 (v 1054-1190) carries it on up-left to the pollard head in the roof's hatching at about (30860-30990, 990-1120). No foot is drawn under the trunk at the fence (reading (b)'s 'tree rooted at the fence's foot at about (31060, 1340)'): the fence's withies run on, unbroken, behind it. The restyle draws the same single leaning willow. The bole's lower edge: I read it at v 1482-1498 across u 31150-31290, as the willows pass does; I agree with its base (31215, 1492, est) and its note on S1.road.main's edge_n. SECOND PASS (willows): the bole's lower edge is drawn at v 1486-1498 across u 31140-31275. Crops: zoom 2.4 greyscale and zoom 3 colour. The bole is the textured mass with bark marks, a pale crack at u 31176-31201 running down to v 1486, and the round hollow at (31255-31284, 1357-1382). Below the edge is a strip of pale ground (v 1498-1510), then the next dark wash (v 1510-1527). Row profiles: at v 1500 the silk turns pale (123-140) from u 31240 east, and at v 1480-1490 from u 31280 east; u 31140-31230 stays dark (100-113) down to v 1500-1505. The base moves from (31240, 1465) est to the middle of that edge. The mass is a tree's bole, not a rock: it has bark texture and a hollow, and diagonal strokes leave its upper west side (31120-31160, 1300-1340) toward the trunk in front of the fence. That fits reading (a). The trunk, head and crown west of u 31100 are the west pass's to settle.

- **A-frame trestle in the hut's fence opening** `S1.prop.trestle_hut_gate_30841`: station +340.1, 2.5 m N; 0.4 m W of thatched hut behind the wattle fences. An A-shaped frame of two crossed sticks (a trestle or a sawhorse) standing on the ground in the opening of the hut's wattle fence, with a short crossbar stroke above it. Nearest: great willow leaning across the hut's fence (hollow bole) 4.5 m SE, large jar with a tied cover, under the shed on posts 7.9 m E, runaway horse, front half lost 9 m N. [identification low] Note: Legs from (30825, 1287) and (30857, 1290), crossing near the apex (30843, 1248); a short stroke at 30827-30840, v 1228. Other reading: a stooping figure (no head or body is painted, so conf low). The restyles read the opening differently (the tile: a pig and a man in blue; the overview: a bow-shaped frame). A thin upright at 30887-30903, v 1227-1277 and a few short strokes on the ground at 30880-30920, v 1283-1297 next to it look like the fence opening's post and a loose piece of fence: left to structures_w. Base = midpoint of the two feet. MERGE: the thin upright and the loose strokes beside it are recorded as the gate post of S1.str.wattle_fence_31100 (points gate_post_foot, gate_post_top).

### Stations +335.0 to +330.0

- **porter of the tomb-sweeping party with a staff** `S1.fig.party_porter_staff_31131`: station +331.5, 16.3 m N; 15.1 m NE of open shed on posts east of the hut; walking; faces W. Adult man, a porter or servant at the east (rear) end of the returning tomb-sweeping party: short jacket belted over trousers, a small cap; walking west with his legs apart, body seen almost from the front, his right arm swinging out to the east. A long thin pole or staff slants up behind his head over his shoulder (its top at about (31110, 314)); nothing hangs from it that the strokes show. (名画记: 杨柳杂花装簇轿顶 (pin 6)) Nearest: walker of the party seen from the front 0.9 m N, the party rider's horse 1.9 m N, rider of the tomb-sweeping party 1.9 m N. [identification med; faded] Note: Feet at about (31112, 445) and (31150, 450); painted height 97 px at foot v 448 (the party's other walkers just west, t2's, are 87-90 px). His west foot is crossed by the load hanging from the pole of the porter just south-west of him (t2's, feet about (31080, 540)), whose pole ends at about (31124, 414) and whose bundle hangs at (31104-31127, 437-483): that bundle is the other porter's, not his. The restyle draws the staff as a stick with a knob at its top and dresses him in a red jacket; colours are the restyle's. He is the only member of the party with feet at u >= 31100; the party's group is t2's (merge joins him to it). Facing read from the stride and the party's direction of travel (west, toward the city); his face turns to the viewer. SECOND PASS (willows): foot and head confirmed at zoom 3.2: feet (31108-31118, 452) and (31146-31155, 452), head (31124-31139, 352-368). The staff runs from his shoulder up-left to (31110, 312). At zoom 8 its top is a small dark knob about 5 x 9 px at (31108-31113, 312-321), with no blade, tines or crossbar. It is a plain staff, or at most a tool seen edge-on. Build a plain staff (carried prop).
- **porter with a shoulder pole behind the sedan** `S1.fig.porter_pole_31083`: station +331.5, 13.9 m N; 12.8 m N of open shed on posts east of the hut; carrying; faces W. Porter in a dark cap and a short belted jacket striding west down the track toward the sedan, a carrying pole on his right shoulder rising to the east, with a rounded load (a basket or a round bundle) hanging at its west end in front of him and a long bundle (of branches, or the paper offerings of the tomb visit?) hanging at its east end. - **the party rider's horse** `S1.ani.horse_party_31023`: station +331.5, 16.8 m N; 16.7 m N of open shed on posts east of the hut; walking; faces E; 0.59 m long. Light-bodied horse drawn in dark outline, walking east with its head and bridle at the east end and its tail raised and flowing at the west end, a saddle cloth under its rider (S1.fig.rider_party_30988). (名画记: 杨柳杂花装簇轿顶 (pin 6)) Nearest: rider of the tomb-sweeping party 0.1 m W, porter with two boxes on a pole, beside the rider 1 m NW, walker of the party seen from the front 1 m S. [identification med] Note: Head 31005-31030, v 268-292, with a dark ear tuft at (31023, 273); tail 30937-30951, v 276-321 (its dark tip at (30949, 321)). Hooves: hind (30959, 352) near and (30993, 340) far; fore (31011, 353) far and (31036, 364) near; fore and hind = the midpoints. It faces east, against the sedan party's westward walk below it: as painted (the rider may be turning back, or the upper track runs east). Read at zoom 3-5 on the scan and on high-pass crops. The overview restyle puts a groom at its head: the strokes there are the horse's head and two forelegs, with no second torso; the tile restyle draws three men. Horse rather than donkey (short ears, long raised tail, size): conf med. SCALE CUE: 97 px from tail to muzzle; back at v ~280, hooves 340-364 (~80 px).
- **willow-decked sedan chair of the tomb-sweeping party** `S1.veh.sedan_willow_30805`: station +331.5, 10.4 m N; 11.1 m N of open shed on posts east of the hut; faces SW; 3.77 m long. Sedan chair decked with willow (杨柳杂花装簇轿顶), carried west-south-west down the upper track: a tall, closed, dark box (its east side darker than its west face) with a low roof and a knob finial; a big spray of about 6-8 long willow twigs fans up and out to the west from the roof's west corner, more willow strands hang down its west face, and a few twigs stand at its east corner. No flowers can be told apart at this size. Carried on poles at shoulder height by a front bearer (west) and a rear bearer (east); no wheels. The passenger is not painted (the box is closed). (名画记: 杨柳杂花装簇轿顶 (pin 6)) Nearest: front sedan bearer 1.6 m SW, rear sedan bearer 1.6 m NE, willow on the bank above the chasers (east of a pair) 2.5 m NW. [est: front, rear; part: the bottom of the box and the bearers' feet among the great willow's whips] Note: Box u 30867-30937; finial (30890, 423); roof eave v 430-443; bottom v 573-587 (bottom west corner (30890, 580), bottom east (30933, 575)). Willow spray 30823-30873, v 415-487 (west); hanging strands 30840-30883, v 450-550; twigs 30933-30950, v 433-467 (east). SCALE CUE: box 70 px wide in u, 155 px from finial to bottom; the bearers are ~115 px tall. One pole line shows, from the front bearer's shoulder (30805, 537) past the box at v 517-530 to the rear bearer's shoulder (31010, 478). DEPTH DIRECTION (the pole line, at shoulder height, not at ground level): (30805, 537) -> (31010, 478), slope dv/du -0.29: the rear end lies north-east of the front, so the sedan heads west-south-west (facing ~205, an estimate from that slope). front/rear = ground under the poles' ends, v from the bearers' feet (est; the front bearer's feet are hidden). The tile restyle stacks jars and a basket on it and adds a child under the poles: invented. sections.md says a woman rides inside; nothing of her is painted, so no figure is recorded.

Willow of the willow_fan form, the east one of the pair: a slender dark trunk rising nearly upright to knobbly heads with fans of long straight bare shoots that spread east over the party's track; early spring, no leaf. - **willow on the bank above the chasers (west of a pair)** `S1.tree.willow_fan_30632`: station +331.5, 9.3 m N; 10.3 m E of small thatched shed behind the stall's east end; about 5.44 m tall; crown 2.34 m wide. Willow of the willow_fan form, the west one of a pair standing 80 px apart on a bank above the chasers: a slender dark trunk leaning a little east forks at about two thirds of its height into knobbly heads that throw fans of long, straight, bare shoots up to near the top of the silk; early spring, no leaf painted. Nearest: willow on the bank above the chasers (east of a pair) 1.5 m E, front sedan bearer 2.6 m S, willow-decked sedan chair of the tomb-sweeping party 3 m SE. Note: Base where the trunk meets the bank line at v ~521 (trunk foot 30622-30652). Fork and heads 30652-30690, v 348-404. Its shoots interlace with those of S1.tree.willow_fan_30712. One trunk. Painted height ~420 px.
- **far pollard willow up the slope** `S1.tree.willow_far_30500`: station +331.5, 14.0 m N; 13.5 m NE of small thatched shed behind the stall's east end; about 2.56 m tall; crown 2.91 m wide. Far willow (willow_fan, a far crown) up the slope north of the chasers' bank: a dark knobbly pollard head throwing a fan of fine bare shoots in all directions; its short trunk is faint. Nearest: small far tree cut by the silk's top edge above the chase field (low confidence) 3.6 m N, willow on the bank above the chasers (east of a pair) 4.4 m S, porter with two boxes on a pole, beside the rider 4.7 m E. [est: base; identification med; faded] Note: Pollard head 30500-30552, v 171-235. Below it a darker smudge runs down-west to about 30480-30510, v 225-270, just above the far fields' diagonal edge (S1.grd.banked_fields_30400 `diag`, second pass: measured at (30370, 266) -> (30550, 381); the line given here before, (30406, 256) -> (30583, 379), has no stroke under its west half): the trunk's foot est there, conf low on the base. The tile restyle draws a man in blue climbing in the tree: the strokes show only the knotted head and its shoots. Read at zoom 4.
- **small far tree cut by the silk's top edge above the chase field (low confidence)** `S1.tree.far_tree_top_30297`: station +331.5, 16.9 m N; 15.4 m NE of small thatched shed behind the stall's east end; about 0.75 m tall; crown 1.92 m wide. A small far tree or bush on the far fields at the top of the silk: a dark stem slanting from its foot up to the north-west, with fans of fine twiggy branches spreading west and up to the silk's top edge, which cuts the crown. Bare, early spring; drawn small (far). Nearest: far pollard willow up the slope 3.6 m S, porter with two boxes on a pole, beside the rider 6.7 m SE, rider of the tomb-sweeping party 7.6 m SE. [est: base; identification low; part: crown cut by the silk's top edge (v ~22-25)] Note: SECOND PASS (west): each tile left these strokes to the other and nobody recorded them: t1 ('Fans at the top right (u 30190-30400, v 20-270) may be this tree's top or the crown of a willow whose trunk stands east of 30300 (t2's): not used', on S1.tree.willow_twin_29970) and t2 ('the branches cut by the top edge at 30175-30300, v 25-108', left to t1). Strokes (zoom 1.8 colour, zoom 3 high-pass): a dark slanting stroke from about (30230, 42) to (30297, 80), with a branching stroke from (30263, 38) down to (30270, 58); a line darkness profile along (30230, 42)->(30297, 80) is darkest on the line itself (red 125 against 145-150 at 6-16 px either side). Fine shoots fan out from it to the west and up to the edge over u 30172-30305, v 22-80, with more faint shoots at u 30170-30260, v 85-145. It is not the top of S1.tree.willow_twin_29970: that tree's top is (30125, 150), and between them (u 30130-30170, v 80-150) the silk is nearly blank. The big fans further south-east (u 30280-30450, v 100-280) are S1.tree.willow_far_30500's west shoots (its crown_w is (30390, 179)), not this tree. Base est at the stroke's lower end, just below the far field line S1.grd.terraced_fields_29450 edge_v63e (proposed, v ~55 here). OTHER READING: twig ends of a willow standing above the top edge (nothing of it is on the silk). The restyle draws a small bare tree with its stem at about (30265, 60-95) here.

The restyle paints a wood pile and a little frame on posts. Recorded as an animal so it is not lost; if the second pass reads a cart or a pile, retire it.

- **twin-stemmed willow east of the stall** `S1.tree.willow_twin_29970`: station +329.0, 4.4 m N; 0.5 m E of small thatched shed behind the stall's east end; about 6.08 m tall; crown 4.55 m wide. Willow with two stems from one foot: a thick knotted trunk leaning strongly east, and a slender stem rising straight up; both end in fans of long rising bare shoots (willow_fan, like the twin-trunk card _3). Leafless early spring. Nearest: leaning willow east of the stall 1.9 m W, wheelbarrow tipped over on the ground 3.9 m W, groom running after the bolting horse (nearest) 4.3 m SE. Note: Foot u 29955-29985, v 600-625. The leaning stem runs up east through (30050, 510) and (30070-30120, 390-460); the straight stem u 29950-29960 from v 600 to ~170. Crown u 29950-30300, v 150-500. The fans at the top right are not this tree's: the ones cut by the top edge (u 30172-30305, v 22-80) are S1.tree.far_tree_top_30297 (second pass), and the ones further south-east (u 30280-30450, v 100-280) are S1.tree.willow_far_30500's west shoots.
- **far tree with a tall straight trunk at the top** `S1.tree.far_tree_top_29428`: station +328.6, 13.2 m N; 8.4 m NE of village stall shed (thatched hip roof, open front); about 1.88 m tall; crown 1.69 m wide. A far tree on the terraced fields at the top of the silk: a tall, straight dark trunk with a flared foot rising to the silk's top edge, its crown of fine shoots cut by the edge (slender form, or a far willow). The restyle paints it as a gnarled willow at (29400-29430, base ~190). The far figures S1.fig.far_figure_29466 / _29482 and the far animal S1.ani.far_ox_29515 stand just east of it.
- **runaway horse, front half lost** `S1.ani.runaway_horse_30075`: station +327.9, on the centre line; 3.5 m NE of tiled farmhouse hall with a veranda; galloping; faces W; 2.08 m long. A horse galloping west at full stretch (the bolting horse of 惊马闯市), its hind legs flung out straight behind it to the east. Only the back, the haunch, the belly line and the hind legs survive, faint; the head, neck and forelegs are lost to damage, with a dark smudge where the chest was. No saddle, bridle or load can be seen on what remains. Three grooms run after it from the east (S1.grp.runaway_horse). (余辉: 惊马闯市) Nearest: groom running after the bolting horse (nearest) 3.9 m NE, leaning willow east of the stall 4.4 m N, twin-stemmed willow east of the stall 4.7 m N. [est: fore; identification med; part: front half lost to damage] Note: Painted extents: back line u 30100-30193, v 763-787 (the haunch's hump at 30155-30195); belly line 30120-30197, v 827-837; hind legs stretched east-south-east to the hooves at (30230, 810) (far leg) and (30240, 855) (near leg); a dark smudge at 30083-30100, v 770-820 (the chest, or the edge of the damage). Nothing of the head, neck or forelegs is painted west of u ~30080. The hooves are off the ground in the gallop: hind = the ground under the near hind hoof (v 858, the level of the near hoof). fore and head are est from a galloping horse's shape (forelegs reaching about 160 px ahead of the hind hooves, head at about the back's height). Painted height back to hoof ~95 px (its chasers are 98-111 px). SCALE CUE: 162 px from the chest smudge (30083) to the hind hooves (30245), legs stretched. The tile restyle redraws it as a small beast with a pack (a pig or a loaded donkey); the strokes show a galloping horse with stretched legs. Read on contrast and high-pass crops at zoom 3.

- **leaning willow east of the stall** `S1.tree.willow_east_29845`: station +326.9, 4.1 m N; inside small thatched shed behind the stall's east end; about 6.47 m tall; crown 4.16 m wide. ### Stations +325.0 to +320.0

- **wheelbarrow tipped over on the ground** `S1.veh.barrow_tipped_29795`: station +325.0, 3.5 m N; 0.1 m SE of village stall shed (thatched hip roof, open front); 1.34 m long. A one-wheeled barrow (独轮车) set down tipped on its side on the open ground in front of the stall's east end: a long tapering trough-like body lying slantwise, its pointed handle end to the west, its spoked wheel upright at the east end. Empty. Nearest: leaning willow east of the stall 2 m E, ox standing with its head lowered 2.9 m W, long stall table (counter) along the open front 3.7 m W. [identification med] Note: Body u 29666-29790, v 633-697; wheel rim u 29777-29807, v 658-694 (about 36 px across, centre (29792, 676): SCALE CUE); a curved frame over the wheel (u 29737-29795, v 635-650). front = under the wheel (the barrow's front end), rear = under the pointed west (handle) end. DEPTH DIRECTION (lying thing, not a ruled edge): (29672, 646) -> (29795, 694), slope +0.39. The wheel is drawn round (face on), so the barrow may be tipped forward onto its nose rather than flat on its side; the setup and the restyle read it as lying on its side.

- **man at the post reaching out to the child (the old man of 惊马闯市)** `S1.fig.man_at_post_29812`: station +321.1, 5.6 m S; 2.6 m W of tiled farmhouse hall with a veranda; leaning; faces W. A man with a topknot, leaning his body against the bar of a tall post and stretching his right arm far out to the west toward the child, as if calling it to him: read as the old man calling a child out of the runaway horse's way. Dressed in a robe; something small (a bundle, a jar or his left hand) at his chest. (余辉: 惊马闯市) Nearest: tall post with a forked top and a crossbar (hitching post or well upright) 0.6 m SE, child standing on the open ground west of the post (惊马闯市) 1.5 m NW, young bare tree in the grove by the vegetable field 4.1 m S. [est: foot; part: legs faint; partly behind S1.prop.post_crossbar_29862's bar] Note: Head u 29805-29843, v 968-993, face to the west; right hand at about (29780, 1035); a small upright shape at his chest (u 29813-29833, v 1027-1057); legs faintly at u 29800-29827, v 1080-1117, foot est at their bottom near the post's foot level (v 1138). The setup's 'figures carrying a pole' at u 29800-29880 are this man and the post. The restyle paints him in grey-blue, leaning on the bar, arm out. Age can't be read (the scholarship calls him old). SECOND PASS (west): read at zoom 3-4 (colour, grey, high-pass): no second figure. The small pointed shape (u 29815-29832, v 1027-1053) has no face, and no second head, arms or feet go with it: it is his left sleeve and hand (or a small gourd or bottle held at his chest, the restyle's hanging sleeve). The dark mass (u 29827-29857, v 1043-1060) lies on the bar's west side, between him and the post: his forearm laid on the bar as he leans. The strokes at v 1080-1117 (u 29800-29837) continue his robe down to his legs, matching his 146 px height.

MERGE (seam): S1's by its base (S2 t12 reads the base at (29070, 1785) and did not record it); its crown runs west over S2 to u ~28585 and its knobbed limb (28720-28880, v 1283-1450) reaches over S2.veh.boat_bottom_28395.

## Groups

- **the village tea and food stall and the animals resting in front** `S1.grp.village_stall` (stations +325.0 to +318.7): The thatched village stall at the edge of the market: a man stands under its west eave by a small table where a customer may sit, a long counter runs along its open front with a round basket at its foot, and in front of it a water buffalo lies and an ox stands with its head down, with a wheelbarrow tipped over to the east: travellers and their animals resting. Members: customer seated at the small table under the stall's west eave (low confidence) (+318.7, 3.5 m N); man standing under the stall's west eave (+319.1, 3.1 m N); small table at the stall's west end (+318.7, 3.3 m N); long stall table (counter) along the open front (+320.4, 3.4 m N); round shallow basket or tub on the ground in front of the stall (+320.3, 3.1 m N); water buffalo lying in front of the stall (+319.2, 0.6 m N); ox standing with its head lowered (+321.8, 2.4 m N); wheelbarrow tipped over on the ground (+325.0, 3.5 m N).
- **two far figures on the terrace at the top** `S1.grp.far_terrace_pair` (stations +326.3 to +326.0): Two tiny far figures crouching or bending together on the terraced fields at the top of the silk, west of the small far willow: farmers at work, perhaps. Members: far small figure on the terrace (west of the far willow), low confidence (+326.0, 14.2 m N); second far small figure on the terrace, low confidence (+326.3, 14.2 m N).
- **far figures with an animal or cart at the top** `S1.grp.far_terrace_ox` (stations +330.1 to +329.5): Two tiny far figures standing beside a long low animal (an ox?) or a small loaded cart on the terraced fields at the top of the silk, just east of the tall far tree. Members: far small figure by the tall far tree, low confidence (+329.5, 13.6 m N); far small figure with a hat by the tall far tree, low confidence (+329.7, 13.6 m N); far long low animal or cart on the terrace (low confidence) (+330.1, 13.6 m N).
- **the runaway horse and its chasers (惊马闯市)** `S1.grp.runaway_horse` (stations +331.5 to +320.0): A horse that has bolted gallops west toward the farmhouse and the village, and three grooms run after it in a line with their arms thrust out: the nearest about 110 px east of its haunch, the other two further east and up the slope behind. West of the farmhouse, on the open ground by the vegetable field, a man leaning on the bar of a tall post stretches his arm toward a small child, calling it out of the horse's way (t1's reading of the old man and the child the scholarship names). Members: runaway horse, front half lost (+327.9, on the centre line); groom running after the bolting horse (nearest) (+331.5, 0.9 m N); groom running after the bolting horse (middle), half behind a willow (+331.5, 2.0 m N); groom running after the bolting horse (farthest east) (+331.5, 6.5 m N); child standing on the open ground west of the post (惊马闯市) (+320.0, 4.9 m S); man at the post reaching out to the child (the old man of 惊马闯市) (+321.1, 5.6 m S); tall post with a forked top and a crossbar (hitching post or well upright) (+321.1, 6.0 m S).
- **travellers on donkeys (迎寒策蹇 / 骑驴客商)** `S1.grp.donkey_travellers` (stations +345.0 to +345.0): Two travellers hooded against the cold ride east on donkeys along the lower track, a man with a staff and a bundle walking between them, a man leading the second donkey by its head, and a man with a stick and a hanging bundle standing at the west end facing them. Members: man with a stick and bundle at the west end of the travellers (+345.0, 9.5 m S); hooded traveller on the first donkey (+345.0, 7.6 m S); first travellers' donkey (+345.0, 7.4 m S); man with a long staff walking between the two riders (+345.0, 5.9 m S); hooded traveller on the second donkey (+345.0, 5.5 m S); second travellers' donkey (+345.0, 5.3 m S); man leading the second donkey (+345.0, 4.3 m S).
- **the tomb-sweeping party coming home (杨柳杂花装簇轿顶)** `S1.grp.tomb_sweeping_party` (stations +331.5 to +331.5): The willow-decked sedan is carried west-south-west down the upper track by two bearers; a porter with a shoulder-pole load and a walker follow behind it to the north-east, and at the top a porter with two boxes on a pole walks beside a man on a horse (horse and porter painted facing east). Members: willow-decked sedan chair of the tomb-sweeping party (+331.5, 10.4 m N); front sedan bearer (+331.5, 8.8 m N); rear sedan bearer (+331.5, 12.0 m N); porter with a shoulder pole behind the sedan (+331.5, 13.9 m N); walker of the party seen from the front (+331.5, 16.6 m N); porter with two boxes on a pole, beside the rider (+331.5, 16.4 m N); rider of the tomb-sweeping party (+331.5, 16.7 m N); the party rider's horse (+331.5, 16.8 m N); porter of the tomb-sweeping party with a staff (+331.5, 16.3 m N).
- **the quiet farmyard** `S1.grp.farmyard_34200` (stations +401.8 to +400.7): The farmyard at the east end of the big thatched farmhouse (寂静的谷场): a single bending figure at the fence with two low objects under the big farmhouse's east hip end; behind the fence the floor lies empty but for the idle roller (S1.prop.roller_frame_34158). Members: bending figure at the yard's fence (+401.8, 1.4 m N); low dark object at the shed's foot (+400.7, 1.3 m N); low elongated object in the farmyard (+401.4, 1.6 m N).
- **the charcoal donkey train (赶驴入城)** `S1.grp.charcoal_train` (stations +435.7 to +429.3): Two drivers bring five donkeys laden with charcoal through the bare trees north of the small bridge, in a file running from the north-west (the rear, with a faint driver behind the last donkey) down to the south-east, where the man with a switch stands at the leading donkey by the big tree at the bridge's east end, leaning back to it with a hand on its load. As painted the donkeys' heads point east and down toward the bridge's east end; the scholarship (赶驴入城) has the train bound for the city, to the west. Members: second charcoal driver at the train's west end (faint) (+429.3, on the centre line); charcoal donkey D1, last in the train (west end) (+430.1, on the centre line); charcoal donkey D2, head hanging low (+430.9, on the centre line); charcoal donkey D3, head hanging low by the big tree (+432.0, on the centre line); charcoal donkey D4, head up and ears pricked (+433.7, on the centre line); charcoal donkey D5, the leader, with woven panniers (+434.8, 0.4 m S); charcoal driver with a switch, at the train's east end (+435.7, on the centre line).

## Open questions

- **Section:** FOR THE LEAD FIRST, THE RECEDING DIRECTION: NO GROUND-LEVEL RECEDING EDGE IS PAINTED IN S1 (structures_w, structures_e, t1 and the frame agent; the rejected candidates are in the derivation). The frame holds S2's seam value to the silk's end: one knot [29050, -51.6], so nothing stretches or folds. It rests on S2's option A (S2's first question: the receding direction turns over in S2's east half). IF THE LEAD TAKES S2's OPTION B (hold +46.6 east of u 25924), S1's knot becomes [29050, 46.6]: mkframe.mjs (scratchpad S1_frame) reads the seam value from map/frames/S2.json, so rerun it, then `scroll_map S1 all` and `map_layout S1`; every far thing in S1 then moves west against the near ones, by 38.9 m at the silk's top (D 30.5 m) and 13.9-20.8 m on the route (D 10.9-16.3 m). Other readings, not used: dx about 0 (the countryside is drawn square on: no farmhouse shows a side wall, the counter and the table are frontal, as 王家纸马铺 is drawn where S2's dx crosses 0), which would move the far things 20.4 m west at the silk's top and 7.3-10.9 m on the route; the stream bank's weak +24 (a curving bank). Any held value keeps everything unfolded; a ramp inside S1 would stretch or squeeze the rows north of the route (from -51.6 at S4b's suggested limit of 1.5 px/m per 100 px it reaches 0 at u ~32490). The strokes can't settle it: the lead decides with S2's turn.
- **Section:** THE STRIPS AND THE DIMINUTION (the one-scale frame is kept; scratchpad S1_frame/strips.py). The route's strip (edge_n to edge_s at the same u, m at 51.25 px of v per metre): the village and the market's edge, u 29050-30350: 6.0-9.5 m; the unpainted bend south, 30400-30720: the route turns across the depth there (not a strip); in front of the hut and the hut willow, 30750-31350: 1.8-4.4 (1.95 at the willow's bole, edge_n moved to the bole's foot); UNDER THE GREAT WILLOWS, 31450-31950: 0.88-1.07 m (the route runs on the 35-50 px strip between the willow dyke's foot and the next bank); the unpainted bend north, 32000-32800: 1.6-5.5; IN FRONT OF THE FARMSTEADS, 33450-34850: 0.84-1.1 m, with nine tree feet in it (next question); the threshing floor and the pale grove, 34900-35900: 1.5-1.9; the charcoal train's path, 35900-36800: 1.5-2.2; the east end, 36850-37390: 0.86-1.27. THE DIMINUTION (standing, walking, running or carrying adults on the ground; painted height over 127 px, 1.65 m at 77 px/m): 0.29-0.35 at foot v 141-145 (the far figures on the field banks); 0.63-0.76 at v 329-448 (the party's porters); 0.81-0.91 at v 541-683 (porters, bearers, the stall man, a groom); 0.65-0.97 at v 766-925 (the charcoal drivers 0.65 and 0.74, the grooms 0.77-0.83, the farmer by the hedge 0.97); 1.10-1.15 at v 1095-1114 (the man with the stick, the man at the post); 1.35-1.49 at v 1476-1627 (the travellers). S1's foreground is painted 1.35-1.5 times life and its far banks a third of life, and the charcoal drivers at the east end are painted smaller than the party's porters 400-500 px higher up: not one curve. The buildings come out under life (checks: the stall shed's eave 1.58 m and front 5.9 m, the hall's eave over its veranda 0.65 m, the barn's eave 0.91 m and front 4.1 m, the threshing floor 5.0 x 2.9 m, the sampan 3.0 m): built at real size in their painted places (BUILDING.md rule 5), as recorded choices; room to walk on the 1 m strips is a build-time choice (build_choices.json).
- **Section:** THE ROUTE THROUGH THE TREES IN FRONT OF THE FARMSTEADS (second pass farms): from u 33400 to 34750 nine tree bases stand inside the route's strip (edge_n on the fences' feet, edge_s on the yard bank's top), five of them on or south of its centre: S1.tree.elm_forked_33433, elm_forked_33785, elm_tall_33865, elm_slender_33906, elm_twin_33936, elm_spreading_34030, bare_elm_twin_34552, bare_elm_twin_34640, bare_elm_tall_34703. The light strip between the fences' feet (v ~895-918) and the yard bank's dark face (v ~943-950) is the only level ground there, and the trees stand on it. The lines are kept as read. The lead's call: the walkable line weaves between the trunks (a recorded build choice), or the centre moves onto the bank top just south of the feet (v ~945-950) with edge_n on the trunk feet. The bench, the straw strip and the crossed poles at 34925-35206 also lie in the strip, where the route already runs through the threshing floor's south part (the merge's note on S1.road.main).
- **Section:** WATER OR GROUND, THE PALE BAND (the ground pilot's question for the user, still open): S1.water.wash_band_34400 is recorded as water (support `stream`, conf low), so the bank feet on its north shore (S1.grd.bank_33650, S1.grd.bank_35200) stand on the stream's level too (support_by, merge_fixes.mjs section 9 (d)). The second pass added evidence: willows, west of u 33000 every pale band carries willows or the route, and the band has no west mouth (if water, it is an inlet whose head is willow B's roots; shore points given in willows.json); start, the band is pale only east of about 35650, and from 34850 to 35850 neither it nor the near bank has an edge on the silk (one even tone at 35450-35750), so it does not visibly join the stream west of ~35650; farms, its edges agree with the bands within 10-25 px. If the user takes it as ground: retire the water entity in merge_fixes.mjs (or give it `ground`), and drop the support_by on bank_33650 and bank_35200 there.
- **Section:** THE CHARCOAL TRAIN'S DIRECTION (second pass start, accepted): as painted, the train walks east-south-east, down toward the bridge's east end. Every readable donkey head is at the east end (D1-D4; D1 is a side view with its head lowered, not end-on), D5 comes toward the viewer (facing 290), and the rear driver holds a stick out east over the last donkey, driving from behind. 赶驴入城 ('driving donkeys into the city', west) is the scholars' title, not a stroke reading. The map records the train as painted; its routine in sections/countryside/people.json is the lead's call.
- **Section:** THE GROUND'S STEPS (the ground pilot): every painted band is a bank one step higher to the north, 35-90 px of face in the foreground and 5-20 px far up. The frame maps every bank line at H 0 (one level), because a band's face mixes depth and height and nothing beside a bank gives its rise, except the stream's banks (0.7 m at the bridge). The steps that matter for the walk are the willow dyke under G1-G3 (S1.grd.bank_31660, about 45 px over the route), the hut's bank (S1.grd.bank_30750) and the yard bank in front of the farmsteads (S1.grd.yard_bank_34400). If the pilot raises bank tops above their feet, each bank's rise is a build choice (or the lead adds a `bank top` support with an H measured on a face), and what stands on a top (the great willows, the hut and its fences, the shed on posts) goes up with it, together.
- **Section:** THE STREAM (support `stream`, H -0.7 below the bank tops, measured at the bridge): the bridge's deck lies on the ground (H 0) and its posts stand in the water (support_by bays); the sampan and the new mooring pole stand on the water; the land tongues and the shoal are at the water's level (both edges waterlines: the builder raises their backs a little as a choice); the feet of the north, west and dyke banks are the shore (support_by bank_foot). The near bank's top (the shores' shore_s) stays on the ground, so the water's south edge maps 1.05 m north of it, the bank's slope between. The two big elms on the west bank's face stand on `bank slope`, H -0.38 and -0.59 from where their feet sit on the face (the tall elm's foot is drawn 10 px above the waterline). East of about u 37250 the water between the east spit and the near bank's end is plain silk (mist): the builder closes the polygon there (start pass). The boat's bow still can't be read (no rudder or sweep): front = the pointed east end, conf low.
- **Section:** THE EAST END: the route's est east end now runs to (37390, 1005), the west edge of the seal at the road's height. The second pass measured the seals as red pixels: u 37248-37503 at v 51-319, 37396-37538 at v 805-1007, 37362-37567 at v 1138-1351, 37424-37584 at v 1597-1756; nothing under a seal is recorded, and the north bank's foot and the east spit show again between and past them to the silk's end (u ~37570-37610). The painted area runs to u 37610; per the brief the ground runs 16 m out under the mist at the east, north and south edges.
- **Section:** OPEN READINGS THAT CHANGE THE BUILD (both readings in each entity's note; the strokes don't settle them): S1.str.fence_brush_33760 (a brushwood fence, or, west of u ~33780, the west hip of a low thatched roof: the hump); S1.bld.farmhouse_long_34950's roof (thatch, or tile: a crisp double eave and scalloped rows); S1.str.posts_35075 (a frame, or an open lean-to; its east post is now the figure S1.fig.figure_in_shed_35108); S1.grd.floor_behind_yard_34285 (a second threshing floor, or plain ground); the far figures and the far ox on the terraces (two standing figures and an ox, or a ploughman at his plough behind an ox); S1.prop.yard_object_34170 and _34208 (baskets or a trough, or hens); S1.ani.pale_bird_34250 (a bird, or an abrasion); the 菜地's planting (13 young trees and a faint 14th: a grove of saplings or a crop, the user's call); S1.tree.sapling_far_31328 (a sapling, a post or a stump); S1.tree.far_willow_34995 (its own far willow, or the near willow's pale west shoots).
- **Section:** SEAM NOTES FOR S2 (S1 does not edit S2's files; the cross-key check within u 28750-29350, scratchpad S1_frame/seamcheck.mjs, found no same-kind pair under 30 px and no line overlapping in u): (1) S2.grd.bank_lines_top_28900's edge_n ends at (29050, 150), and S1.grd.terraced_fields_29450's edge_v155 now starts there exactly (it was 2 px off; merge_fixes copies the point from S2's strokes): one stroke split at the seam, not a duplicate; (2) the curved far field line west of the seam, (29000, 81) -> (29050, 106), confirmed by the second pass (west) on a line profile, is not recorded by S2; S1's new edge_v128w continues it east from (29050, 110); (3) S2's edge_s ends at (29050, 192, est) with no S1 line east of the seam (its note says the stroke runs to 29073), and S1's edge_v57 runs west of the seam to about (28960, 58), where S2 has no line; (4) the route's three points at u 29050 are the same in both maps (S1 copies S2's on every run), and the painted areas meet at (267.21, -4.39) and (290.62, 30.54).

- `S1.fig.party_porter_staff_31131` (porter of the tomb-sweeping party with a staff): Feet at about (31112, 445) and (31150, 450); painted height 97 px at foot v 448 (the party's other walkers just west, t2's, are 87-90 px). His west foot is crossed by the load hanging from the pole of the porter just south-west of him (t2's, feet about (31080, 540)), whose pole ends at about (31124, 414) and whose bundle hangs at (31104-31127, 437-483): that bundle is the other porter's, not his. He is the only member of the party with feet at u >= 31100; the party's group is t2's (merge joins him to it). Facing read from the stride and the party's direction of travel (west, toward the city); his face turns to the viewer. SECOND PASS (willows): foot and head confirmed at zoom 3.2: feet (31108-31118, 452) and (31146-31155, 452), head (31124-31139, 352-368). The staff runs from his shoulder up-left to (31110, 312). At zoom 8 its top is a small dark knob about 5 x 9 px at (31108-31113, 312-321), with no blade, tines or crossbar. It is a plain staff, or at most a tool seen edge-on. Build a plain staff (carried prop).

- `S1.veh.boat_sampan_36440` (small empty boat (sampan) moored under the small bridge): Outline only, in thin lines: a vertical stroke for the west end's transom (u 36175-36183, v 1131-1150); the far gunwale (36180, 1132) -> (36293, 1157) -> (36440, 1180); the near gunwale (36193, 1160) -> (36327, 1190) -> (36437, 1190); the bottom's curve (36353, 1210) -> (36440, 1187) up to the pointed end; a thwart tick at (36397, 1175-1180). SCALE CUE: hull 262 px long and 30-40 px deep, at v 1130-1210. front = the pointed east end, which the line ties to the bridge (S1.str.small_bridge_36365, a post foot at about (36445, 1100)); no rudder or sweep shows, so which end is the bow is a reading (conf low on it). BOTH READINGS of the water: afloat in the stream at the bank's edge (support 'stream', taken: the mooring line, and the plain silk round it is the same as under the bridge) or drawn up on the bank (the bank's wash strokes run under its west half, (36207, 1180) -> (36333, 1227)). The restyle draws it about 30 px south-east of the outline, on dry ground with grass: not used. No crew, so no group. MERGE: the rope's far end is the foot of a post of S1.str.small_bridge_36365 (its bays, at about (36445, 1100)). No rudder, sweep or side sweeps are painted, so the lead's bow rule (rudder or sweep = rear) cannot be applied: front stays the pointed east end tied to the bridge, conf low. SECOND PASS (start): AFLOAT, confirmed. The hull lies wholly on pale silk. Its near gunwale (36193, 1160) -> (36327, 1190) runs 20-35 px north of S1.grd.spit_under_boat_36140's north edge (36250, 1210) -> (36335, 1228). The dark stroke under its west half, (36207, 1180) -> (36327, 1223), is that spit's edge, not ground under the boat. The west transom (36173-36187, 1127-1160) is about 70 px east of the west bank's foot (S1.grd.bank_36060: (36105, 1110), (36075, 1180)). Support stream stands. BOW: still unreadable. No rudder, sweep or side sweep is drawn; the west end is a raised square transom and the east end lower and pointed. front = east stays, conf low. THE LINE at its east end is vertical at u 36442-36446, v about 1125-1180. It stops 40-60 px below the nearest bridge post foot (36455, 1063), and no post stands at (36445, 1100). It is proposed as its own prop, S1.prop.mooring_pole_36444 (a pole in the stream bed the boat is tied to). If accepted, read 'the line' in this note and in `what` as that pole.

Readings: (a, kept) one great leaning willow rooted in the bole (the restyle draws exactly this); (b) the bole is a separate cut stump or a rock on the bank, and the trunk at u 31010-31100 belongs to a tree rooted at the fence's foot at about (31060, 1340) (t2's range), whose crown is the same whips. On zoom 1.6 and 2.5 crops the trunk is one band: from the bole's upper west side (31150, 1345) diagonal strokes run up-left to the fence's foot at (31046-31102, 1297-1335); a pale band with a dark west edge rises at u 31008-31046 from v 1310 to the hut's eave (v ~1122); above the eave a dark trunk section at u 30902-30940 (v 1054-1190) carries it on up-left to the pollard head in the roof's hatching at about (30860-30990, 990-1120). No foot is drawn under the trunk at the fence (reading (b)'s 'tree rooted at the fence's foot at about (31060, 1340)'): the fence's withies run on, unbroken, behind it. The restyle draws the same single leaning willow. The bole's lower edge: I read it at v 1482-1498 across u 31150-31290, as the willows pass does; I agree with its base (31215, 1492, est) and its note on S1.road.main's edge_n. SECOND PASS (willows): the bole's lower edge is drawn at v 1486-1498 across u 31140-31275. Crops: zoom 2.4 greyscale and zoom 3 colour. The bole is the textured mass with bark marks, a pale crack at u 31176-31201 running down to v 1486, and the round hollow at (31255-31284, 1357-1382). Below the edge is a strip of pale ground (v 1498-1510), then the next dark wash (v 1510-1527). - `S1.prop.mooring_pole_36444` (upright pole at the small boat's east end (mooring pole?)): SECOND PASS (start): t8 named this line in the boat's `what` and note as 'its mooring rope (or a pole stuck in the stream bed)' running 'up to the foot of a bridge post at about (36445, 1100)', and the merge wrote that into S1.str.small_bridge_36365's note. On the strokes it does not reach a post. Row profiles (1-px columns, 10 px bands, blur 1.2) find a dark line at u 36442-36446 from v 1130 to 1180 (9-15 levels below the band's median), possibly from about 1115 (a broad weak minimum at 36437). There is nothing at v 1070-1110 (depth 3-11 at the band's edge only). The nearest post feet are S1.str.small_bridge_36365's bays (36455, 1063), (36480, 1067), (36491, 1065): 40-60 px of plain silk lie between the line's top and them. It is vertical (no slant toward any post) and straight, and it stops at the boat's end below and at nothing above, so it is not a silk crease. A rope tied to a post would reach the post and slant, so the reading taken is a pole planted in the stream bed beside the boat's east end, like the mooring posts S2 and S3 record standing in the water (S2.prop.mooring_post_23126, S3.prop.mooring_post_17702: support water; here the stream). Base est at the waterline at the boat's end; its foot is under water. If the lead keeps the rope reading, decline this and correct the boat's and the bridge's notes instead (the 'post foot at (36445, 1100)' has no post: see the changes). Crops: boat_z3.jpg, rope_z5g.jpg, ov_bridge.jpg, bridge_z16.jpg.

READINGS: (a) a brushwood fence or hedge round the farmstead (the crossed short strokes differ from the smoother roof wash above them); (b) the lower thatch of the houses coming down almost to the ground. The rounded HUMP at its west end (u 33400-33540, top (33491, 793)) is drawn the same way; the restyle paints it as a golden haystack: if it is a free-standing straw stack, it is t5's prop (listed in my report). At zoom 1.7-2.6 (hump_hp.jpg, hump_z.jpg, h1_norm.jpg) its outline is one crisp, smooth line from (33430, 845) up through (33500, 800) to (33560, 778), where it meets S1.bld.farmhouse_gable_33670's west eave corner (33575, 772). The surface inside is the same short slanted dashes as the rest of the band and as the thatch on the gable house's east slope (33680-33760, v 780-850), with no boundary between them. The band's lower edge is a straight dark line at v 867-881 (33430-33780), with a paler strip below it (v 881-899) and then the ground line. OTHER READINGS of the route: (a) an upper way behind the hut and the great willows at v about 850-1000 (the willows' crowns hide that ground; it would skip the willows' feet, 隋堤烟柳's view); (b) the travellers' path (S1.road.path_30300) reached from the village ground through the 54 px gap between the 菜地's east end and the farmhouse wing (u 30002-30056, v 1130-1300): too narrow for a road; (c) under the bottom-left willow past the walker at 29200: nothing drawn. The sedan party's track (S1.road.path_30950) leaves the silk at its top and cannot carry the route east. The east piece's own reading follows. HOW READ. u 33400-35250 (conf med): edge_n = the feet of the brushwood fences and hedges (v 882-918) and the barn's posts (v 867-880); edge_s = the top of the yard bank S1.grd.yard_bank_34400 (v 945-966; 925-935 at the threshing floor). u 36190-36900 (conf med): the centre is the line of the donkey train's hooves and drivers' feet (t8: head driver ~(36245, 766), donkeys at v 798, 830-841, 880-885, 903-917, 936-946, rear driver (36795, 925)); edge_s = the top of the dyke bank S1.grd.dyke_36380 under them; edge_n is est, ~50 px north of the centre. u 35250-36190: not painted as a path; est, joining the threshing floor to the train's head behind (north of) the big bare trees whose bases are at v ~1100-1230. u 32850-33300: under the willow crown, est; structures_w's reading of S1.road.main at u 33000-33150 decides, and the merge reconciles the overlap. East of the rear driver nothing marks it: it is est, bent to pass south of the roots of the bare tree with the leaning pole (t8, roots u 37030-37180, v 880-955) and north of the north bank's top. East end (37240, 975) est: from u 37250 a seal covers the strip (v 800-980) and beyond it is plain silk (mist), so nothing past it is recorded. WHY THIS IS THE MAIN ROAD: the charcoal donkey train, the painting's travellers here, walks it toward the city, and it is the only continuous level strip through the farmsteads; the bridge path S1.road.path_36380 has no readable continuation off the bridge. Width 40-80 px of v (about 1-1.5 m at 51.25 px/m). SECOND PASS (start): the note says 'from u 37250 a seal covers the strip (v 800-980)'. Measured, the seal at that height spans u 37396-37538, v 805-1007. The seal starting at 37248 is the top one (v 51-319). Between 37250 and 37396 the strip is painted open ground: the bank's foot (S1.grd.bank_37050, v 962-978) above it and the north bank's top (S1.grd.bank_36900, v ~1045-1060) below it. The est route can run on to the seal's edge, ending at 37390. That is OPTIONAL: it is still a reading through unpainted ground, and it adds about 2 m to the walkable east end. The centre (31150, 1530, est) is unchanged. The lines are kept as read (the strokes don't settle it): the walkable line weaves between the trunks (a build choice), or the lead moves the centre onto the bank top just south of the feet (v ~945-950).

The restyle paints all of it as dry ground with grass and a stone-edged channel; never follow it here. SECOND PASS (start): EXTENTS. shore_s is the near bank's top edge (S1.grd.bank_south_34400, same points). It runs on east to about 37250 and fades there; it is not lost at 36850. Its west end stays at u 36000, where it meets S1.water.wash_band_34400; the near bank's dark band itself begins at about 35900. shore_n follows the north bank's lowest stroke, which runs on east between the seals to the silk's end (S1.grd.bank_36900, below). The strip v 1007-1138 is covered by none of them. East of about 37250 the water between the east spit (S1.grd.spit_east_37060) and the near bank's end is plain silk (mist): close the polygon there, at the builder's choice.
- `S1.water.wash_band_34400` (pale band below the farmsteads: water or a low flat): BOTH READINGS (the lead or the user decides; the builder must not settle it silently). WATER: it is blank silk like the stream at the bridge (S1.water.stream_36500), it runs on from it at u ~36000, the land tongues S1.grd.spit_36150 and S1.grd.spit_36225 point east into it, and the scholars put water at the scroll's start; the great willows west of my range (隋堤烟柳, the Sui embankment) would then stand along its bank. GROUND: no ripple lines are drawn anywhere in it; the big willow at u ~32620-32820 west of my range has its roots at v ~1560-1600 on the band's south edge as if standing on its surface; and the restyle draws dry ground (it draws dry ground everywhere, though). shore_n: the foot of the dark bank S1.grd.bank_33650 (u 32850-34450), est round that bank's east end (u 34450-34650), then the foot of S1.grd.bank_35200 (u 34650-35700) and the south edge of the land tongue S1.grd.spit_36225 (u 35800-36000). shore_s: the top of the near bank S1.grd.bank_south_34400. At u 34150-34850 the two banks almost touch (v 1490-1505): only a thin pale strip connects the two halves. West of u 33000 it continues into structures_w's range (v ~1490-1600), where no water entity is recorded; structures_w notes the other way round that the DARK band v 1450-1600 at u 33100-33600 might be water or a hollow. Seen at zoom 3 (insp_lens.jpg) that dark band is made of tongue-shaped wash strokes pointing into the pale silk, like the land tongues by the bridge, so I read the pale silk as the water and the dark as land. MERGE: west of u 33000 nothing continues this band: structures_w recorded the ground there as banks (S1.grd.bank_32905, foot v 1486, and S1.grd.bank_32700, v 1580-1612, which runs inside this band's v range), and it reads the DARK band (v 1450-1600 from 32850 east) as the possible water, where this entity takes the PALE band. Water against ground is the ground pilot's open question for the user; the builder must not settle it silently. SECOND PASS (start), u 35400-36000 only: the lowest dark wash stroke above the band, which is the west end of S1.grd.spit_36225's layer, ends at v ~1385-1395 from 35500 to 35850. The step to the paler silk below measures 1392 at 35500 (weak, 96 -> 113), 1385-1390 at 35560-35700 (101 -> 120-125) and 1393 at 35780. So the shore is about 60 px lower there than the 1330 mapped from S1.grd.bank_35200's foot. At 35420 the dark ends at 1342. EXTENT: the band is pale only east of about 35650. At 35450-35600 the silk below v 1340 is one even mid-tone (104-115) down to the silk's edge, with no pale band and no near bank (see S1.grd.bank_south_34400). In this range the band's south shore (35000-35900) is drawn on no stroke. That is more evidence for the water-or-ground question: the pale band does not visibly join the stream's water west of about 35650. FRAME AGENT: shore_s's last two points move onto the near bank's top as the second pass (start) measured it, (35900, 1590) and (36000, 1582) (were 1592 est at both), so this band's south shore meets S1.water.stream_36500's shore_s at u 36000.
